<h1 id="antvg6-lite">@antv/g6-lite</h1>
<h2 id="classes">Classes</h2>
<h3 id="eventemitter">EventEmitter</h3>
<p>Defined in: <a href="https://github.com/antvis/G6Lite/blob/89fda20d39fb37f22a2ad46dd9a05ea461889f5a/src/events.ts#L28">events.ts:28</a></p>
<p>@antv/g6-lite
A high-performance graph analysis engine based on WebGL, designed for large-scale graph data analysis.</p>
<p>Public API surface is being re-baselined during the 0.1.0 beta period.
Engine internals are excluded from the package root and published API
declarations; consume supported APIs only through this entry point.</p>

<figure class="shiki not-prose api-code"><div class="api-code-viewport"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">updateNodePosition</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span></span>

<p>With <code>anchor: start</code>, overlap by CSS pixels or half the boundary-adjacent badge height.</p>

<h3 id="extensionfactory-1">extensionFactory()</h3>
<figure class="shiki not-prose api-code"><div class="api-code-viewport"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">function</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> extensionFactory</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">T</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">>(</span><span style="color:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">factory</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> ExtensionFactory</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">T</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">>)</span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> ExtensionFactory</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">T</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">>;</span></span></code></pre></div></figure>

<p>Defined in: <a href="https://github.com/antvis/G6Lite/blob/89fda20d39fb37f22a2ad46dd9a05ea461889f5a/src/layout/runLayout.ts#L222">layout/runLayout.ts:222</a></p>
<p>Run the configured layout on graph data; returns nodes with positions.
Registered extensions and built-in functional layouts take precedence over
worker and @antv/layout execution. Worker failures fall back to the same
main-thread library path.</p>
